# RA8P1 independent review findings

**Initial review and individual adjudication complete; scoped r018 software verification recorded, not an exhaustive support sign-off.** All 228 initial findings were recorded before fixes; the checkpoint records unchanged hashes for all 4,473 frozen inputs. Fourteen supplemental findings were recorded before their respective fixes, for **242 IDs: 238 Confirmed, 1 Rejected, 3 Unresolved**. Remediation accounting is **229 fixed in the stated software scope, 12 open (including the 3 unresolved source gaps), 1 rejected**. Final host testing passed 889 tests with stable inputs; fresh both-core mode/default builds, source/ELF checks and provenance-preserving older-device regressions are linked in the [r018 report](evidence/final-r018-summary.md). Cross-owner aggregation and unresolved coverage are reported separately; no hardware qualification is claimed. Original allegations and source-limited/partial qualifications are retained below. Frozen initial locations and hashes: [coverage](coverage.md), [snapshot](evidence/initial-snapshot.json). Primary HM means R01UH1064EJ0130 Rev.1.30. Paths are project-relative; initial lines refer to the frozen snapshot even if later moved; supplemental rows identify their later review scope.

Disposition (Confirmed / Rejected / Unresolved) is separate from remediation status. Rejected allegations remain in this ledger. Hardware remains unverified throughout.
